def mult_anneal(a0, beta_schedule, iters, t_dead=None, best_score=None):
    a=normalize_sum(a0,1.0)
    best_a=a.copy(); best_sc=score(a)
    eta=1.0
    for beta in beta_schedule:
       for it in range(iters):
          if t_dead and time.monotonic()>t_dead: break
          b=conv_h(a)
          M=float(b.max())
          if M<=0: break
          # p LSE
          ex=np.exp(beta*(b-M)); s=ex.sum(); p=ex/s
          g=gradient_from_weights(p,a)  # length n
          m= float(np.dot(a,g))  # because sum1
          d=g-m
          # choose step eta; ensure factor not huge
          # scale d by its max or norm
          scale=float(np.max(np.abs(d)))
          if scale<1e-14: break
          # try steps = eta / (1+scale?) We can set base = 0.1*(1/scale) maybe.
          for step in [0.01,0.05,0.1,0.2,0.5]: # if step/scale
              shift=step*d/scale? Better: α=step/scale to make max change step.
              factor=np.exp(-α*d)
              # if factor under/overflow; α*d can be large negative => factor huge. Clip.
              ad=α*d
              ad=np.clip(ad, -50,50)
              an=a*np.exp(-ad)
              an=np.maximum(an,0); S=an.sum(); if S<... continue
              an/=an.sum()
              sc=score(an)
              if sc<current_best: accept? We can just choose best candidate among steps.
          If best_sc improved set a=best_candidate, report.
          If no improvement over it, reduce scale/div? 
       # adaptive: if progress then keep eta else reduce beta? no.
    return best_a,best_score

def score(a):
   a=np.asarray(a,float)
   a=np.max(a,0)
   S=a.sum()
   if S <= 1e-12: return inf
   b=conv_h(a)
   return 2.0*a.size*float(b.max())/(S*S)
```

L-BFGS call:
```
def run_lbfgs(a0, beta, maxiter, maxfun, t_deadline=None):
    n=len(a0)
    S0=np.sqrt(2.0*n)
    a0=np.clip(a0,0,1000)
    S=a0.sum()
    if S<1e-6: a0[:] = S0/n
    else: a0 = a0.copy(); a0 *= S0/S
    bounds=[(0.0,1000.0)] * n
    opts={'maxiter':maxiter,'maxfun':maxfun,'ftol':1e-15,'gtol':1e-14,'maxls':40}
    # If time, we cannot pass deadline. Use small maxfun.
    res=minimize(lse_obj, a0, args=(beta,S0,1e-6), method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, bounds=bounds, options=opts)
    a=np.clip(res.x,0,1000)
    sc=score(a)
    return sc,a
```
Top-level `lse_obj(x, beta, S0, lam): return lse_score_grad(x,beta,S0,lam)`. But `minimize` calls with x only; args pass.

def gradient_from_weights(p,a):

def spectral_flat(a, max_iters=100, gamma=0.2, deadline=None):
    n=len; S0=...; a=normalize_sum(a,S0); M=...; D=...
    best_a=a; best_sc=score(a)

       if time and >deadline: break
       A=np.fft.rfft(a,M)
       B=irfft(A*A,M)[:2*n-1] # or conv_h(a)
       Bmix=(1-gamma)*B+gamma*np.tile? D scalar shape 2n-1.
       Bmix=np.maximum(Bmix,0)
       QB=np.fft.rfft(Bmix,M)
       with errstate: ratio=QB/(A*A + 1e-12*(np.abs(A*A)+1))
       ratio[~np.isfinite(ratio)]=1
       sqrt_ratio=np.sqrt(ratio)
       A_new=A*sqrt_ratio
       new=np.fft.irfft(A_new,M)[:n].real
       new=np.maximum(new,0)
       if new.sum()<1e-12: break

           a=best_a.copy()
       else:
           a=new
    return best_a,best_sc

Build matrix:
```
def build_conv_matrix(f):
    n=f.size
    rows=[]; cols=[]; vals=[]
    for k in range(2*n-1):
        j0=max(0,k-n+1); j1=min(k,n-1)
        if j1>=j0:
           cols_k=np.arange(j0,j1+1)
           vals_k=f[k-cols_k]
           rows_k=np.full_like(cols_k,k)
           rows.append(rows_k); cols.append(cols_k); vals.append(vals_k)
    return csr_matrix((vals, (rows,cols)), shape=(2*n-1,n))
```

def _as_nonneg(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a=np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a=np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0)
    return a

def normalize_sum(a,target):
    a=_as_nonneg(a)
    S=a.sum()
    if S <= 1e-14:
       return np.full(a.size, target/max(a.size,1))
    return a * (target/S)
```

`conv_h(a)`:
```
def conv_h(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64)
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return np.array([])
    L=2*n-1
    M=1 << (L-1).bit_length()
    A=np.fft.rfft(a, M)
    return np.fft.irfft(A*A, M)[:L]
```
If n=1, L-1=0 bit_length0 ->1. good.

`score(a)`:
```
def score(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a=np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a=np.maximum(a,0)
    S=a.sum()
    if S <= 1e-12:
       return np.inf
    b=conv_h(a)
    return float(2.0*a.size*float(b.max())/(S*S))
```

`gradient_from_weights(p,a)`: as above. Need p length 2n-1. If p has negative? no.

```
def gradient_from_weights(p,a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return np.array([])
    L=3*n-2
    M=1 << (L-1).bit_length()
    RA=np.fft.rfft(a[::-1], M)
    P=np.fft.rfft(np.asarray(p,dtype=np.float64), M)
    conv_res=np.fft.irfft(RA*P, M)[n-1:2*n-1]
    return 2.0*conv_res
```
Test n=1: L=1,M=1, slice [0:1] okay.

`lse_score_grad(x,beta,S0,lam)`:
```
def lse_score_grad(x,beta,S0,lam):
    x=np.asarray(x,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n=x.size
    x=np.where(np.isfinite(x),x,0)
    x=np.maximum(x,0)
    S=x.sum()
    if S <= 1e-12:
       return 1e10, np.zeros(n)
    b=conv_h(x)
    M=float(b.max())
    if beta < 1e-8:
       p=np.full(2*n-1,1.0/(2*n-1))
       L=float(b.mean())
    else:
       r=beta*(b-M)
       ex=np.exp(np.clip(r,-745,0.0)) # r<=0
       s=ex.sum()
       if s <= 0: ...
       p=ex/s
       L=M + float(np.log(s))/beta
    gradL=gradient_from_weights(p,x)
    S2=S*S; S3=S2*S
    grad=(2.0*n)*gradL/S2 - (4.0*n*L)/S3
    if lam>0:
       grad = grad + (2.0*lam*(S-S0))*np.ones(n)
    obj=2.0*n*L/S2 + lam*(S-S0)*(S-S0)
    return float(obj), grad
```
Clipping r lower -745 prevents underflow; exp(0)=1. If beta large, s might be very small? no at max exp0. If multiple max, okay. If beta*(b-M) negative -large, exp 0. Use `np.exp(np.minimum(r,0.0))`; no need clip lower. Underflow safe.

`run_lbfgs(a0,beta,maxfun,maxiter,S0=None)`:
```
def run_lbfgs(a0,beta,maxfun=1500,maxiter=800):
    a0=np.asarray(...)
    n=a0.size
    if S0 is None: S0=np.sqrt(2.0*n)
    x=normalize_sum(a0,S0)
    # if lambda maybe 1e-7
    lam=1e-6
    bounds=[(0.0,1000.0)]*n
    opts={'maxiter':maxiter,'maxfun':maxfun,'ftol':1e-15,'gtol':1e-13,'maxls':40}
    try:
       res=minimize(lse_score_grad,x,method='L-BFGS-B',args=(beta,S0,lam),jac=True,bounds=bounds,options=opts)
       x=np.asarray(res.x,dtype=np.float64)
    except Exception:
       x=a0.copy()
    x=np.asarray(x); x=np.where(np.isfinite(x),x,0); x=np.clip(x,0,1000)
    x=normalize_sum(x,S0)
    try: sc=score(x)
    except: sc=np.inf
    return sc,x
```
If L-BFGS fails due older scipy callback? okay.

`mult_anneal(a0,beta_schedule,iters_per,deadline)`:
Use `S0=np.sqrt(2n)`.
Implement with stored current score from b.
```
def mult_anneal(a0,beta_schedule,iters_per,deadline):
    a=normalize_sum(a0,S0)
    current=score(a); best_a=a; best_sc=current
    no_improve=0
    for beta in beta_schedule:
       for it in range(iters_per):
          if time.monotonic() > deadline: return best_a,best_sc
          b=conv_h(a); S=a.sum(); M=b.max(); cur_sc=2n*M/(S*S)
          r=beta*(b-M); ex=np.exp(r); s=ex.sum(); p=ex/s
          g=gradient_from_weights(p,a)
          m=float(np.dot(a,g))/S
          shift=g-m
          sc=float(np.max(np.abs(shift)))
          if sc<1e-14: break
          alpha=0.2/sc
          improved=False
          for fac in [1.0,0.5,0.25,0.125]:
             ad=alpha*fac*shift
             ad=np.clip(ad,-50,50)
             an=a*np.exp(-ad)
             an=np.maximum(an,0)
             an=normalize_sum(an,S0)
             sc_new=score(an)
             if sc_new < cur_sc - 1e-12:
                 a=an; cur_sc=sc_new; improved=True
                 if sc_new < best_sc: best_sc=sc_new; best_a=a.copy()
                 break
          if improved: no_improve=0
          else:
             no_improve +=1
             if no_improve > 10:
                # restart from best and maybe lower beta? break beta
                a=best_a.copy(); cur_sc=best_sc; no_improve=0
                # maybe break to next beta
                break
       no_improve=0
    return best_a,best_sc
```
This calls score (conv) each trial; if many failed, up to 4 conv per iter. Might be heavy. We can reduce trials to 2. Use `[1.0,0.3]`. Or use LSE candidate score? Since we already have b for current; for candidate, compute b. We'll just exact. Use 2 trials to save. If no improvement, a=best maybe. Multiplicative may be slow. Limit iters. We'll set iters_per=80 for beta three =>240 iterations, each maybe 1-3 conv. n=10k conv ~0.02, 240*2=500 conv=10s? okay.

`spectral_flat(a0,max_iters,deadline,gamma)`:

Implementation:
```
def spectral_flat(a0,max_iters,deadline,gamma=0.15):
   a=normalize_sum(a0,S0); best...
   for it in range(max_iters):
      if time> deadline: break
      A=np.fft.rfft(a,M)
      B=np.fft.irfft(A*A,M)[:2n-1]
      D=np.full(2n-1, S*S/(2n-1))
      Bmix = (1-gamma)*B + gamma*D
      Bmix=np.maximum(Bmix,0)
      QB=np.fft.rfft(Bmix,M)
      with errstate: ratio=QB/(A*A+1e-12*(np.abs(A*A)+1.0))
      ratio=np.where(np.isfinite(ratio),ratio,1.0)
      sqrt_ratio=np.sqrt(ratio)
      Anew=A*sqrt_ratio
      new=np.fft.irfft(Anew,M)[:n].real
      new=np.maximum(new,0)
      if new.sum()<=...: break
      new=normalize_sum(new,S0)
      sc=score(new)
      if sc<best_sc: best...
      if sc <= best_sc + 0.005:
         a=new
      else:
         a=best_a.copy()
   return best_a,best_sc
```
Can use target D not scalar? Maybe shape to avoid endpoints: D=mean constant. Good.

def build_initializations(n, rng, parent, include_parent=True):
   inits=[]
   if parent is not None:
      if len(parent)==n: inits.append(parent.copy())
      else: inits.append(np.interp(np.linspace(0,1,n), np.linspace(0,1,len(parent)), parent))
   x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
   inits.append(np.ones(n))
   inits.append(1.0 + 0.6*(2*x-1)**2)
   inits.append(1.0 + 0.8*np.cos(2*np.pi*x)) # uniform? maybe
   inits.append(1.0 + 0.5*np.sin(2*np.pi*x))
   # two block
   s=0.5
   inits.append(1.0 + s*(np.where(x<0.5, 1, -1)))
   # four block
   inits.append(1.0 + 0.5*np.cos(4*np.pi*x)) # cosine high at 0,.5,1? nonneg if amp<=1? min0.5. good.
   inits.append(1.0 + 0.5*np.cos(8*np.pi*x))
   # endpoint singular gentle
   eps=1e-3
   inits.append(1.0 + 2.0*(x+eps)/(1.0) **? no)
```

    # L-BFGS

       a_p=...
       sc,a_p=run_lbfgs(a_p,2000,maxfun=500)
       update
       trials++
    return best_a,best_sc

Let's implement safe output:
```
def safe_finalize(a):
   a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
   a=np.where(np.isfinite(a),a,0)
   a=np.clip(a,0,1000)
   m=a.max()
   if m<=1e-15:
      return [0.5]*64
   out=a*(1000.0/m)
   out=np.clip(out,0,1000)
   if out.sum()<0.02:
       return [0.5]*64
   # Ensure list of Python floats
   return [float(x) for x in out]
```

Functions:
```
def as_finite_nonnegative(a, upper=None):
    a=np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size ==0: return a
    a=np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
    if upper is not None:
       a=np.minimum(a, float(upper))
    return a
```
But internal values may need upper None. Output uses upper=1000.

`normalize_sum`:
```
def normalize_sum(a,target):
    a=as_finite_nonnegative(a)
    n=a.size
    S=float(a.sum())
    if S <= 1e-14 or n==0:
       return np.full(max(n,1), target/max(n,1), dtype=np.float64)
    return a*(target/S)
```
If n==0, full(1)? Should not.

`score`:
```
def score(a):
    a=as_finite_nonnegative(a)
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return np.inf
    S=float(a.sum())
    if S<=1e-12: return np.inf
    b=conv_h(a)
    return float(2.0*n*float(b.max())/(S*S))
```

if p.size < Lp: p=np.pad(p,(0,Lp-p.size))
else: p=p[:Lp]

def mult_anneal(a0,beta_schedule,iters_per,deadline):

`build_conv_matrix`:
```
def build_conv_matrix(f):
    n=f.size
    rows=[]; cols=[]; vals=[]
    for k in range(2*n-1):
       j0=max(0,k-n+1); j1=min(k,n-1)
       if j1>=j0:
          cols_k=np.arange(j0,j1+1,dtype=np.int32)
          vals_k=f[k-cols_k].astype(np.float64)
          rows_k=np.full(cols_k.size,k,dtype=np.int32)
          rows.append(rows_k); cols.append(cols_k); vals.append(vals_k)
    if not rows: return csr_matrix((2*n-1,n))
    r=np.concatenate(rows); c=np.concatenate(cols); v=np.concatenate(vals)
    return csr_matrix((v,(r,c)),shape=(2*n-1,n))
```
`linprog` options `time_limit` maybe not accepted by some solvers? Highs accepts? Could include try omit if fails. To be safe, options={'time_limit': 10}. If linprog errors due option, catch and retry with options={}. Inside try if first fails, second.
```
try:
   res=linprog(..., options={'time_limit': 5})
   if res is None or not res.success and not hasattr(res,'x'): res=linprog(..., options={})
except Exception:
   res=linprog(..., method='highs')
```
But high memory. We'll simple:
```
try:

def build_initializations(n,rng,parent=None,limit=8):
   inits=[]
   x=np.linspace(0.1,0.9,n) ? If use 0,1 endpoints for interp. Use 0,1.
   if parent is not None: ...
   inits.append(np.ones(n))
   inits.append(1.0+0.8*(2*x-1)**2)
   inits.append(1.0+0.5*np.cos(2*np.pi*x))
   inits.append(1.0+0.5*np.sin(2*np.pi*x))
   inits.append(1.0+0.5*(np.where(x<0.5,1,-1)))
   inits.append(1.0+0.5*np.cos(4*np.pi*x))
   inits.append(1.0+0.5*np.cos(8*np.pi*x))
   w=max(0.01, 0.05)
   inits.append(1.0+2.0*np.exp(-x/(w+1e-6))+2.0*np.exp(-(1-x)/(w+1e-6)))
   for amp in (0.15,0.35):
      z=rng.standard_normal(n)
      z=np.cumsum(np.cumsum(z))
      z=z-z.mean()
      std=z.std()
      if std>1e-12: z=z/std
      z=np.tanh(2.0*z)
      inits.append(1.0+amp*z)
   for nb in (8,16,32):
      per=max(1,n//nb)
      vals=1.0+0.4*rng.standard_normal(nb)
      vals=np.clip(vals,0.1,10.0)
      a=np.repeat(vals,per)
      if a.size<n: a=np.concatenate([a, vals[:n-a.size]])
      inits.append(a[:n])
   out=[]
   for arr in inits:
      arr=as_finite_nonnegative(arr)
      arr=np.maximum(arr,1e-6)
      if arr.size==n and arr.sum()>0:
         out.append(arr)
      if len(out)>=limit: break
   return out
```

`np.sin` at endpoints zero; fine.

`interp_sequence(src,n)`:
```
def interp_sequence(src,n):
   src=as_finite_nonnegative(src)
   if src.size==n: return src.copy()
   x_old=np.linspace(0,1,src.size)
   x_new=np.linspace(0,1,n)
   return np.interp(x_new,x_old,src)
```

def subgradient_polish(a0, max_iters=500, deadline=None, lower_bound=1.0):
    a=normalize_sum(a0,S0)
    best...

    stall=0

      if cur<best_sc: best...

      j0=max(0,k-n+1); j1=min(k,n-1)
      g=np.zeros(n)
      g[j0:j1+1] = 2.0*a[j0:j1+1][::-1]
      m=np.dot(a,g)/S
      d=g-m
      norm=float(np.linalg.norm(d))
      if norm<1e-12: break
      step=(cur-lower_bound)/(norm*norm + 1e-12)
      step=min(max(step,1e-8),0.05)
      an=a - step*d
      an=np.maximum(an,0); an=normalize_sum(an,S0)
      sc=score(an)
      if sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
          best... stall=0
      else:
          stall +=1
          if stall > 40: break
          a=best_a.copy()
      a=an if sc<cur+1e-8 else a # not sure

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    t0=time.monotonic()
    total=max(60.0,float(budget_s))
    hard_deadline=t0+total-30.0
    rng=np.random.default_rng(int(seed))
    parent=None
    try:
       p = globals().get('height_sequence_1', None)
       if p is not None:
          p=np.asarray(p,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
          p=np.where(np.isfinite(p),p,0.0)
          p=np.clip(p,0.0,1000.0)
          if p.size >= 64 and p.sum()>1e-8:
              parent=p
    except Exception:
       parent=None
    best_a=None; best_sc=np.inf
    if parent is not None:
       try:
          sc=score(parent)
          if np.isfinite(sc): best_a=parent.copy(); best_sc=sc
       except: pass
       print(f"Parent length={len(parent)} score={best_sc:.8f}")
    seeds=[]
    seen_lengths=set()
    if parent is not None:
       n0=len(parent)
       if n0>20000:
          a_opt=interp_sequence(parent,16000)
          seeds.append(('parent_down', a_opt, int(0.55*total)))
          # also keep parent as best but not optimize? output maybe parent if down worse. Add a seed with parent? too long.
       else:
          seeds.append(('parent', parent.copy(), int(0.55*total)))
       seen_lengths.add(n0 if n0<=20000 else 16000)
       for mult in (1.3,1.65):
          n=int(n0*mult)
          if 1024 <= n <= 20000 and n not in seen_lengths:
             a=interp_sequence(parent,n)
             seeds.append((f'parent_up_{n}', a, int(0.18*total)))
             seen_lengths.add(n)
    # LP seed
    if HAVE_LP and hard_deadline - time.monotonic() > 600:
       n_lp=512
       if parent is not None:
          lp_base=interp_sequence(parent,n_lp)
       else:
          lp_base=np.ones(n_lp)
       lp_deadline=min(hard_deadline, time.monotonic()+120.0)
       a_lp,sc_lp=lp_refine(lp_base,iters=30,deadline=lp_deadline)
       for n_out in (8192,12288):
          if n_out not in seen_lengths and hard_deadline-time.monotonic()>300:
             a=interp_sequence(a_lp,n_out)
             seeds.append((f'lp_{n_out}', a, int(0.12*total)))
             seen_lengths.add(n_out)
    # synthetic seeds
    for n in (4096,8192):
       if n in seen_lengths: continue
       if hard_deadline-time.monotonic() > 300:
          inits=build_initializations(n,rng,parent,limit=4)
          for idx,init in enumerate(inits[:2]):
             seeds.append((f'syn_{n}_{idx}', init, int(0.08*total)))
             if len(seeds)>8: break
       if len(seeds)>10: break
    if not seeds:
       if parent is None:
          a0=np.ones(4096)
       else:
          a0=np.ones(min(4096, len(parent)))
       seeds=[('default', normalize_sum(a0, np.sqrt(2*a0.size)), int(total))]
    # Process seeds
    cumulative_budget=0.0
    for label,a0,budget in seeds:
       if budget <=0: continue
       cumulative_budget += budget
       limit=min(hard_deadline, t0 + cumulative_budget)
       if limit <= time.monotonic(): continue
       try:
          sc_local, a_local = optimize_sequence(a0, limit, rng, label)
       except Exception as e:
          print(f"{label} failed: {e}")
          continue
       if np.isfinite(sc_local) and (best_a is None or sc_local < best_sc):
          best_sc=sc_local
          best_a=np.asarray(a_local,dtype=np.float64).copy()
          print(f"GLOBAL NEW BEST {best_sc:.8f} from {label} n={len(best_a)} t={time.monotonic()-t0:.0f}s")
       if time.monotonic() > hard_deadline - 60:
          break
    if best_a is None:
       best_a=np.ones(1024)
    out=safe_finalize(best_a)
    try:
       verified=float(evaluate_sequence(out))
    except Exception:
       verified=float(best_sc if best_a is not None else np.nan)
    print(f"FINAL internal={float(best_sc) if best_a is not None else np.nan:.8f} verified={verified:.8f} length={len(out)}")
    return out
```

`as_finite_nonnegative(a, upper=None)`:
```
def as_finite_nonnegative(a, upper=None):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return a
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    if upper is not None:
        a = np.minimum(a, float(upper))
    return a
```

```
def solve_lp_once(c,A,b,bounds):
    try:
      res=linprog(c,A_ub=A,b_ub=b,bounds=bounds,method='highs',options={'time_limit':4})
    except Exception:
      try:
        res=linprog(c,A_ub=A,b_ub=b,bounds=bounds,method='highs')
      except Exception:
        res=linprog(c,A_ub=A,b_ub=b,bounds=bounds)
    return res
```

def subgradient_polish(a0, max_iters=500, deadline=None, lower_bound=None):
    a=as_finite_nonnegative(a0); n=a.size
    S0=np.sqrt(2.0*n); a=normalize_sum(a,S0)
    cur=score(a); best_a=a.copy(); best_sc=cur
    if lower_bound is None: lower_bound = max(1.0, 2.0*n/float(2*n-1))
    stall=0
    for it in range(max_iters):
       if deadline is not None and time.monotonic()>deadline: break
       b=conv_h(a); S=float(a.sum()); if S<=... break
       M=float(b.max()); cur=2n*M/(S*S)
       if cur < best_sc: best... stall=0
       else: stall +=1? Better stall counts nonimprovement relative best.
       k=int(np.argmax(b))
       j0=...; j1=...
       g=np.zeros(n); if j1>=j0: g[j0:j1+1]=2*a[j0:j1+1][::-1]
       m=np.dot(a,g)/S
       d=g-m
       norm=np.linalg.norm(d)
       if norm<=1e-14: break
       step=(cur-lower_bound)/(norm*norm+1e-30)
       step=min(max(step,1e-8),0.05)
       an=a - step*d
       an=np.maximum(an,0); an=normalize_sum(an,S0)
       sc=score(an)
       if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
           best...; stall=0; a=an
       else:
           stall +=1
           if sc < cur + 1e-8:
               a=an
           else:
               a=best_a.copy()
           if stall > 50:
               break
    return best_a,best_sc
```

Let's write optimize_sequence with phases and time calculations:
```
def optimize_sequence(a0, deadline, rng, label):
    a=as_finite_nonnegative(a0)
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return np.array([]), np.inf
    S0=np.sqrt(2.0*n)
    a=normalize_sum(a,S0)
    best_a=a.copy(); best_sc=score(a)
    print(f"[{label}] n={n} start={best_sc:.8f}")
    now=time.monotonic()
    # spectral
    spec_budget = min(90.0, max(15.0, 0.05*(deadline-now)))
    if n <= 12000 and now + spec_budget < deadline:
       a_sp, sc_sp = spectral_flat(a, max_iters=35, deadline=now+spec_budget, gamma=0.12)
       if np.isfinite(sc_sp) and sc_sp < best_sc:
          best_sc=sc_sp; best_a=a_sp.copy()
          a=a_sp
       elif np.isfinite(sc_sp) and sc_sp <= best_sc + 0.02:
          a=a_sp
    # mult
    now=time.monotonic()
    mult_budget = min(240.0, max(30.0, 0.12*(deadline-now)))
    if now + mult_budget < deadline:
       a_mp, sc_mp=mult_anneal(a, [20,100,500], iters_per=50, deadline=now+mult_budget)
       if np.isfinite(sc_mp) and sc_mp < best_sc: update
       elif np.isfinite(sc_mp) and sc_mp <= best_sc+0.005: a=a_mp
    # lbfgs
    now=time.monotonic()
    if n <= 6000: maxfun=1600
    elif n <= 10000: maxfun=1100
    else: maxfun=800
    betas=[30,120,500,2000]
    for beta in betas:
       now=time.monotonic()
       if now > deadline - 120: break
       sc,a_opt=run_lbfgs(a,beta,maxfun=maxfun,maxiter=maxfun)
       if np.isfinite(sc):
          if sc < best_sc:
             best_sc=sc; best_a=a_opt.copy(); a=a_opt
          elif sc <= best_sc + 1e-5:
             a=a_opt
    # final mult
    now=time.monotonic()
    if now + 120 < deadline:
       final_budget=min(120.0, max(20.0, 0.07*(deadline-now)))
       a_hi,sc_hi=mult_anneal(a,[1500,3000], iters_per=30, deadline=now+final_budget)
       if ... update
    # subgradient
    now=time.monotonic()
    if now + 150 < deadline and n <= 16000:
       sg_budget=min(150.0, max(30.0,0.07*(deadline-now)))
       a_sg,sc_sg=subgradient_polish(a, max_iters=1000, deadline=now+sg_budget)
       update/close use
    # perturbations
    trials=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 150 and trials < 3:
       ...
    return best_a,best_sc
```
The `if now + mult_budget < deadline` ensures if not enough remaining skip. Good.
Perturbation loop: use `deadline - 150` so L-BFGS maxfun maybe 60s. Use remaining.
Perturb generation:
```
a_p=best_a.copy()
if trials==0:
   noise=rng.standard_normal(n)
   noise=np.cumsum(noise)
   noise=noise - noise.mean()
   noise=np.tanh(noise/(noise.std()+1e-12))
   a_p *= (1.0 + 0.05*noise)
elif trials==1:
   b=conv_h(best_a); k=int(b.argmax()); j0=max(0,k-n+1); j1=min(k,n-1)
   seg=best_a[j0:j1+1]; contrib=seg[::-1].copy()
   nn=len(seg)
   top_count=max(5, min(nn, n//100))
   top=np.argsort(contrib)[::-1][:top_count]
   total=0.0
   for li in top:
      gi=j0+li
      red=a_p[gi]*0.05
      a_p[gi]=max(0.0,a_p[gi]-red)
      total += red
   bottom=np.argsort(contrib)[:max(1, nn-top_count)]
   if total>0 and bottom.size>0:
      add=total/bottom.size
      for li in bottom:
         a_p[j0+li] += add
else:
   x=np.arange(n,dtype=np.float64)/float(n)
   freq=1.0 + 8.0*rng.random()
   phase=2.0*np.pi*rng.random()
   amp=0.03+0.04*rng.random()
   a_p *= (1.0 + amp*np.cos(2.0*np.pi*freq*x + phase))
a_p=np.maximum(a_p,0); a_p=normalize_sum(a_p,S0)
sc,a_opt=run_lbfgs(a_p,2000,maxfun=600,maxiter=600)
update
trials +=1
```

sc,a_opt=run_lbfgs(a,beta,maxfun=call_maxfun,maxiter=call_maxfun)
```

```python
"""
Optimizer for non-negative sequences minimizing
    2 n max_k (a*a)_k / (sum a)^2.

The program uses a hybrid continuous-discrete search:
  * LSE-smoothed objective with analytic gradient,
  * L-BFGS-B continuation in beta,
  * exponentiated-gradient/multiplicative active-lag updates,
  * spectral flattening toward a flat self-convolution target,
  * exact subgradient polishing of the max-convolution term,
  * optional small linear-program directions to create diverse basins,
  * interpolation/re-interpolation across sequence lengths.

The best valid sequence found before the time budget expires is returned.
"""

import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize

try:
    from scipy.optimize import linprog
    from scipy.sparse import csr_matrix
    HAVE_LP = True
except Exception:
    HAVE_LP = False


def as_finite_nonnegative(a, upper=None):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return a
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    if upper is not None:
        a = np.minimum(a, float(upper))
    return a


def normalize_sum(a, target):
    a = as_finite_nonnegative(a)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return a
    if not np.isfinite(target):
        target = 1.0
    S = float(a.sum())
    if S <= 1e-14:
        return np.full(n, target / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
    return a * (float(target) / S)


def conv_h(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    M = 1 << (L - 1).bit_length()
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, M)
    return np.fft.irfft(A * A, M)[:L]


def score(a):
    a = as_finite_nonnegative(a)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.inf
    S = float(a.sum())
    if S <= 1e-12:
        return np.inf
    b = conv_h(a)
    return float(2.0 * n * float(np.max(b)) / (S * S))


def gradient_from_weights(p, a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)

    Lp = 2 * n - 1
    if p.size != Lp:
        if p.size < Lp:
            p = np.concatenate([p, np.zeros(Lp - p.size, dtype=np.float64)])
        else:
            p = p[:Lp]

    L = 3 * n - 2
    M = 1 << (L - 1).bit_length()
    RA = np.fft.rfft(a[::-1], M)
    P = np.fft.rfft(p, M)
    conv_res = np.fft.irfft(RA * P, M)[n - 1:2 * n - 1]
    return 2.0 * conv_res


def lse_score_grad(x, beta, S0, lam):
    x = as_finite_nonnegative(x)
    n = x.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.inf, np.array([], dtype=np.float64)

    S = float(x.sum())
    if S <= 1e-12:
        return 1e10, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    b = np.maximum(conv_h(x), 0.0)
    M = float(np.max(b))

    beta = float(beta)
    if beta < 1e-8:
        Lp = 2 * n - 1
        p = np.full(Lp, 1.0 / float(Lp), dtype=np.float64)
        L = float(np.sum(b)) / float(Lp)
    else:
        r = beta * (b - M)
        ex = np.exp(np.clip(r, -745.0, 0.0))
        s = float(np.sum(ex))
        if s <= 1e-300:
            Lp = 2 * n - 1
            p = np.full(Lp, 1.0 / float(Lp), dtype=np.float64)
            L = float(np.sum(b)) / float(Lp)
        else:
            p = ex / s
            L = M + float(np.log(s)) / beta

    gradL = gradient_from_weights(p, x)
    gradL = np.where(np.isfinite(gradL), gradL, 0.0)

    S2 = S * S
    S3 = S2 * S
    grad = (2.0 * n) * gradL / S2 - (4.0 * n * L) / S3

    if lam > 0.0:
        grad = grad + (2.0 * float(lam) * (S - S0)) * np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)

    obj = (2.0 * n * L) / S2 + float(lam) * (S - S0) * (S - S0)
    if not np.isfinite(obj):
        return 1e10, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    grad = np.where(np.isfinite(grad), grad, 0.0)
    return float(obj), grad


def run_lbfgs(a0, beta, maxfun=1000, maxiter=1000):
    a0 = as_finite_nonnegative(a0)
    n = a0.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.inf, a0

    S0 = np.sqrt(2.0 * n)
    x = normalize_sum(a0, S0)

    lam = 1e-6
    bounds = [(0.0, 1000.0)] * n
    opts = {
        "maxiter": int(max(maxiter, 10)),
        "maxfun": int(max(maxfun, 10)),
        "ftol": 1e-15,
        "gtol": 1e-12,
        "maxls": 60,
    }

    try:
        res = minimize(
            lse_score_grad,
            x,
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            args=(float(beta), float(S0), float(lam)),
            jac=True,
            bounds=bounds,
            options=opts,
        )
        x = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
    except Exception:
        x = a0.copy()

    x = np.where(np.isfinite(x), x, 0.0)
    x = np.clip(x, 0.0, 1000.0)
    x = normalize_sum(x, S0)
    sc = score(x)
    return sc, x


def mult_anneal(a0, beta_schedule, iters_per, deadline):
    a0 = as_finite_nonnegative(a0)
    n = a0.size
    if n == 0:
        return a0, np.inf

    S0 = np.sqrt(2.0 * n)
    a = normalize_sum(a0, S0)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = score(a)

    if deadline is None:
        deadline = np.inf

    for beta in beta_schedule:
        no_improve = 0
        for it in range(int(iters_per)):
            if time.monotonic() > deadline:
                break

            b = np.maximum(conv_h(a), 0.0)
            S = float(a.sum())
            if S <= 1e-12:
                break
            M = float(np.max(b))
            cur_sc = float(2.0 * n * M / (S * S))

            r = float(beta) * (b - M)
            ex = np.exp(np.clip(r, -745.0, 0.0))
            ss = float(np.sum(ex))
            if ss <= 1e-300:
                p = np.full(2 * n - 1, 1.0 / float(2 * n - 1), dtype=np.float64)
            else:
                p = ex / ss

            g = gradient_from_weights(p, a)
            g = np.where(np.isfinite(g), g, 0.0)
            m = float(np.dot(a, g)) / S
            shift = g - m
            scale = float(np.max(np.abs(shift)))
            if scale < 1e-14:
                break

            alpha = 0.2 / scale
            improved = False
            for fac in (1.0, 0.35, 0.12):
                ad = alpha * fac * shift
                ad = np.clip(ad, -50.0, 50.0)
                an = a * np.exp(-ad)
                an = np.maximum(an, 0.0)
                an = normalize_sum(an, S0)
                sc_new = score(an)
                if np.isfinite(sc_new) and sc_new < cur_sc - 1e-12:
                    a = an
                    cur_sc = sc_new
                    improved = True
                    if sc_new < best_sc:
                        best_sc = sc_new
                        best_a = a.copy()
                    break

            if improved:
                no_improve = 0
            else:
                no_improve += 1
                if no_improve >= 12:
                    a = best_a.copy()
                    no_improve = 0
                    break

    return best_a, best_sc


def spectral_flat(a0, max_iters, deadline, gamma=0.12):
    a0 = as_finite_nonnegative(a0)
    n = a0.size
    if n == 0:
        return a0, np.inf

    S0 = np.sqrt(2.0 * n)
    a = normalize_sum(a0, S0)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = score(a)

    L = 2 * n - 1
    M = 1 << (L - 1).bit_length()
    Dval = (S0 * S0) / float(L)

    if deadline is None:
        deadline = np.inf

    for it in range(int(max_iters)):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline:
            break

        A = np.fft.rfft(a, M)
        B = np.maximum(np.fft.irfft(A * A, M)[:L], 0.0)
        Bmix = (1.0 - gamma) * B + gamma * Dval
        Bmix = np.maximum(Bmix, 0.0)

        QB = np.fft.rfft(Bmix, M)
        denom = A * A
        denom_safe = denom + 1e-12 * (np.abs(denom) + 1.0)

        with np.errstate(divide="ignore", invalid="ignore"):
            ratio = QB / denom_safe

        ratio = np.where(np.isfinite(ratio), ratio, 1.0 + 0.0j)
        mag = np.abs(ratio)
        phase = np.angle(ratio)
        mag = np.clip(mag, 1e-10, 1e10)
        ratio = mag * np.exp(1j * phase)

        sqrt_ratio = np.sqrt(ratio)
        A_new = A * sqrt_ratio
        new = np.fft.irfft(A_new, M)[:n].real
        new = np.maximum(new, 0.0)

        if float(new.sum()) <= 1e-14:
            break

        new = normalize_sum(new, S0)
        sc = score(new)

        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_a = new.copy()

        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc <= best_sc + 0.02:
            a = new
        else:
            a = best_a.copy()

    return best_a, best_sc


def build_conv_matrix(f):
    f = np.asarray(f, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = f.size
    rows = []
    cols = []
    vals = []

    for k in range(2 * n - 1):
        j0 = max(0, k - n + 1)
        j1 = min(k, n - 1)
        if j1 >= j0:
            cols_k = np.arange(j0, j1 + 1, dtype=np.int32)
            vals_k = f[k - cols_k]
            rows_k = np.full(cols_k.size, k, dtype=np.int32)
            rows.append(rows_k)
            cols.append(cols_k)
            vals.append(vals_k)

    if len(rows) == 0:
        return csr_matrix((2 * n - 1, n), dtype=np.float64)

    r = np.concatenate(rows)
    c = np.concatenate(cols)
    v = np.concatenate(vals)
    return csr_matrix((v, (r, c)), shape=(2 * n - 1, n), dtype=np.float64)


def solve_lp_once(c, A, b, bounds):
    try:
        return linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=b, bounds=bounds, method="highs",
                       options={"time_limit": 4})
    except Exception:
        try:
            return linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=b, bounds=bounds, method="highs")
        except Exception:
            return linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=b, bounds=bounds)


def lp_refine(a0, iters=20, deadline=None):
    if not HAVE_LP:
        a0 = as_finite_nonnegative(a0)
        return a0, score(a0)

    a0 = as_finite_nonnegative(a0)
    n = a0.size
    if n == 0 or n > 1500:
        return a0, score(a0)

    S0 = np.sqrt(2.0 * n)
    a = normalize_sum(a0, S0)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = score(a)

    U = max(1000.0, 10.0 * S0)

    if deadline is None:
        deadline = np.inf

    for it in range(int(iters)):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline:
            break

        cur_sc = score(a)
        f = as_finite_nonnegative(a) + 1e-12
        bf = np.maximum(conv_h(f), 0.0)
        M = float(np.max(bf))
        if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
            break

        A = build_conv_matrix(f)
        c = -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        b_ub = np.full(2 * n - 1, M, dtype=np.float64)
        bounds = [(0.0, U)] * n

        try:
            res = solve_lp_once(c, A, b_ub, bounds)
        except Exception:
            break

        g = getattr(res, "x", None)
        if g is None:
            break

        g = as_finite_nonnegative(g)
        Sg = float(g.sum())
        if Sg <= 1e-12 or not np.isfinite(Sg):
            break

        gn = g * (S0 / Sg)
        local_a = a
        local_sc = cur_sc

        for t in (1.0, 0.7, 0.4, 0.2):
            an = (1.0 - t) * a + t * gn
            an = as_finite_nonnegative(an)
            an = normalize_sum(an, S0)
            sc = score(an)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < local_sc:
                local_sc = sc
                local_a = an

        if local_sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = local_sc
            best_a = local_a.copy()

        if local_sc < cur_sc:
            a = local_a

    return best_a, best_sc


def interp_sequence(src, n):
    src = as_finite_nonnegative(src)
    m = src.size
    if n <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    if m == n:
        return src.copy()
    if m == 0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if m == 1:
        return np.full(n, float(src[0]), dtype=np.float64)
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m, dtype=np.float64)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    return np.interp(x_new, x_old, src).astype(np.float64)


def build_initializations(n, rng, parent=None, limit=8):
    inits = []
    if n <= 0:
        return inits

    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)

    if parent is not None:
        inits.append(interp_sequence(parent, n))

    inits.append(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))
    inits.append(1.0 + 0.8 * (2.0 * x - 1.0) ** 2)
    inits.append(1.0 + 0.5 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * x))
    inits.append(1.0 + 0.5 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * x))
    inits.append(1.0 + 0.5 * np.where(x < 0.5, 1.0, -1.0))
    inits.append(1.0 + 0.5 * np.cos(4.0 * np.pi * x))
    inits.append(1.0 + 0.5 * np.cos(8.0 * np.pi * x))

    w = 0.05
    inits.append(1.0 + 2.0 * np.exp(-x / (w + 1e-9)) + 2.0 * np.exp(-(1.0 - x) / (w + 1e-9)))

    for amp in (0.15, 0.35):
        z = rng.standard_normal(n)
        z = np.cumsum(np.cumsum(z))
        z = z - z.mean()
        std = float(z.std())
        if std > 1e-12:
            z = z / std
        z = np.tanh(2.0 * z)
        inits.append(1.0 + amp * z)

    for nb in (8, 16, 32):
        per = max(1, n // nb)
        vals = 1.0 + 0.4 * rng.standard_normal(nb)
        vals = np.clip(vals, 0.1, 10.0)
        a = np.repeat(vals, per)
        if a.size < n:
            a = np.concatenate([a, vals[:n - a.size]])
        inits.append(a[:n].astype(np.float64))

    out = []
    for arr in inits:
        arr = as_finite_nonnegative(arr)
        arr = np.maximum(arr, 1e-6)
        if arr.size == n and float(arr.sum()) > 1e-14:
            out.append(arr)
        if len(out) >= limit:
            break

    return out


def subgradient_polish(a0, max_iters=500, deadline=None, lower_bound=None):
    a0 = as_finite_nonnegative(a0)
    n = a0.size
    if n == 0:
        return a0, np.inf

    S0 = np.sqrt(2.0 * n)
    a = normalize_sum(a0, S0)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = score(a)

    if lower_bound is None:
        lower_bound = max(1.0, (2.0 * n) / float(2 * n - 1))

    if deadline is None:
        deadline = np.inf

    stall = 0
    for it in range(int(max_iters)):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline:
            break

        b = np.maximum(conv_h(a), 0.0)
        S = float(a.sum())
        if S <= 1e-12:
            break
        M = float(np.max(b))
        cur_sc = float(2.0 * n * M / (S * S))

        if cur_sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = cur_sc
            best_a = a.copy()
            stall = 0

        k = int(np.argmax(b))
        j0 = max(0, k - n + 1)
        j1 = min(k, n - 1)

        g = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        if j1 >= j0:
            g[j0:j1 + 1] = 2.0 * a[j0:j1 + 1][::-1]

        m = float(np.dot(a, g)) / S
        d = g - m
        norm = float(np.linalg.norm(d))
        if norm <= 1e-14:
            break

        step = (cur_sc - lower_bound) / (norm * norm + 1e-30)
        step = min(max(step, 1e-8), 0.05)

        an = a - step * d
        an = np.maximum(an, 0.0)
        an = normalize_sum(an, S0)
        sc_new = score(an)

        if np.isfinite(sc_new) and sc_new < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc_new
            best_a = an.copy()
            stall = 0

        if np.isfinite(sc_new) and sc_new < cur_sc + 1e-9:
            a = an
            stall = max(0, stall - 1)
        else:
            stall += 1
            a = best_a.copy()

        if stall > 80:
            break

    return best_a, best_sc


def optimize_sequence(a0, deadline, rng, label):
    a0 = as_finite_nonnegative(a0)
    n = a0.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64), np.inf

    S0 = np.sqrt(2.0 * n)
    a = normalize_sum(a0, S0)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = score(a)

    print(f"[{label}] n={n} start={best_sc:.8f}", flush=True)

    now = time.monotonic()

    spec_budget = min(90.0, max(15.0, 0.05 * max(0.0, deadline - now)))
    if n <= 12000 and now + spec_budget < deadline:
        a_sp, sc_sp = spectral_flat(a, max_iters=35, deadline=now + spec_budget, gamma=0.12)
        if np.isfinite(sc_sp) and sc_sp < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc_sp
            best_a = a_sp.copy()
            a = a_sp
            print(f"[{label}] spectral={sc_sp:.8f}", flush=True)
        elif np.isfinite(sc_sp) and sc_sp <= best_sc + 0.02:
            a = a_sp

    now = time.monotonic()
    mult_budget = min(240.0, max(30.0, 0.12 * max(0.0, deadline - now)))
    if now + mult_budget < deadline:
        a_mp, sc_mp = mult_anneal(a, [20, 100, 500], iters_per=50, deadline=now + mult_budget)
        if np.isfinite(sc_mp) and sc_mp < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc_mp
            best_a = a_mp.copy()
            a = a_mp
            print(f"[{label}] mult={sc_mp:.8f}", flush=True)
        elif np.isfinite(sc_mp) and sc_mp <= best_sc + 0.005:
            a = a_mp

    if n <= 6000:
        maxfun_global = 2500
        est_sec = 0.045
    elif n <= 10000:
        maxfun_global = 1800
        est_sec = 0.065
    else:
        maxfun_global = 1200
        est_sec = 0.095

    for beta in (30, 120, 500, 2000):
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now + 120.0 > deadline:
            break

        call_maxfun = int(min(maxfun_global, max(200, (deadline - now - 120.0) / max(est_sec, 1e-6))))
        if call_maxfun < 200:
            break

        sc, a_opt = run_lbfgs(a, beta, maxfun=call_maxfun, maxiter=call_maxfun)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            if sc < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc
                best_a = a_opt.copy()
                a = a_opt
                print(f"[{label}] L-BFGS beta={beta} score={sc:.8f}", flush=True)
            elif sc <= best_sc + 1e-5:
                a = a_opt

    now = time.monotonic()
    if now + 120.0 < deadline:
        final_budget = min(120.0, max(20.0, 0.07 * max(0.0, deadline - now)))
        a_hi, sc_hi = mult_anneal(a, [1500, 3000], iters_per=30, deadline=now + final_budget)
        if np.isfinite(sc_hi) and sc_hi < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc_hi
            best_a = a_hi.copy()
            a = a_hi
            print(f"[{label}] high-mult={sc_hi:.8f}", flush=True)
        elif np.isfinite(sc_hi) and sc_hi <= best_sc + 0.004:
            a = a_hi

    now = time.monotonic()
    if now + 150.0 < deadline and n <= 16000:
        sg_budget = min(150.0, max(30.0, 0.07 * max(0.0, deadline - now)))
        a_sg, sc_sg = subgradient_polish(a, max_iters=1200, deadline=now + sg_budget)
        if np.isfinite(sc_sg) and sc_sg < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc_sg
            best_a = a_sg.copy()
            a = a_sg
            print(f"[{label}] subgrad={sc_sg:.8f}", flush=True)
        elif np.isfinite(sc_sg) and sc_sg <= best_sc + 0.003:
            a = a_sg

    trials = 0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 150.0 and trials < 3:
        now = time.monotonic()
        a_p = best_a.copy()

        if trials == 0:
            noise = rng.standard_normal(n)
            noise = np.cumsum(noise)
            noise = noise - noise.mean()
            std = float(noise.std())
            if std > 1e-12:
                noise = np.tanh(noise / std)
            a_p *= (1.0 + 0.05 * noise)
        elif trials == 1:
            b = np.maximum(conv_h(best_a), 0.0)
            k = int(np.argmax(b))
            j0 = max(0, k - n + 1)
            j1 = min(k, n - 1)
            seg = a_p[j0:j1 + 1]
            contrib = seg[::-1].copy()
            nn = len(seg)
            top_count = max(5, min(nn, n // 100))
            top = np.argsort(contrib)[::-1][:top_count]

            total = 0.0
            for li in top:
                gi = j0 + int(li)
                red = float(a_p[gi]) * 0.05
                a_p[gi] = max(0.0, float(a_p[gi]) - red)
                total += red

            bottom = np.argsort(contrib)[:max(1, nn - top_count)]
            if total > 0.0 and bottom.size > 0:
                add = total / float(bottom.size)
                for li in bottom:
                    a_p[j0 + int(li)] += add
        else:
            xx = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
            freq = 1.0 + 8.0 * rng.random()
            phase = 2.0 * np.pi * rng.random()
            amp = 0.03 + 0.04 * rng.random()
            a_p *= (1.0 + amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * freq * xx + phase))

        a_p = np.maximum(a_p, 0.0)
        a_p = normalize_sum(a_p, S0)

        call_maxfun = int(min(800, max(120, (deadline - now - 120.0) / max(est_sec, 1e-6))))
        if call_maxfun < 120:
            break

        sc, a_opt = run_lbfgs(a_p, 2000, maxfun=call_maxfun, maxiter=call_maxfun)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_a = a_opt.copy()
            a = a_opt
            print(f"[{label}] perturb#{trials} score={sc:.8f}", flush=True)

        trials += 1

    return best_a, best_sc


def safe_finalize(a):
    a = as_finite_nonnegative(a, upper=1000.0)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return [0.5]

    m = float(np.max(a))
    if m <= 1e-15:
        return [0.5] * min(n, 64)

    out = a * (1000.0 / m)
    out = np.where(np.isfinite(out), out, 0.0)
    out = np.clip(out, 0.0, 1000.0)

    if float(out.sum()) < 0.02:
        return [0.5] * min(n, 64)

    return [float(y) for y in out]


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    total = max(60.0, float(budget_s))
    hard_deadline = t0 + total - 30.0
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))

    parent = None
    try:
        p = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
        if p is not None:
            p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
            p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
            if p.size >= 64 and float(p.sum()) > 1e-8:
                parent = p
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    best_a = None
    best_sc = np.inf

    if parent is not None:
        try:
            sc = score(parent)
            if np.isfinite(sc):
                best_a = parent.copy()
                best_sc = sc
                print(f"Parent length={len(parent)} score={sc:.8f}", flush=True)
        except Exception:
            pass

    seeds = []
    seen = set()

    if parent is not None:
        n0 = int(len(parent))
        opt_len = min(n0, 18000)
        base = parent.copy() if n0 <= 18000 else interp_sequence(parent, opt_len)
        first_budget = max(180, int(0.55 * total))
        first_budget = min(first_budget, int(total))
        seeds.append(("parent", base, first_budget))
        seen.add(opt_len)

        for mult in (1.30, 1.65):
            n = int(round(n0 * mult))
            if 1500 <= n <= 20000 and n not in seen:
                a = interp_sequence(parent, n)
                seeds.append((f"parent_up_{n}", a, max(120, int(0.16 * total))))
                seen.add(n)

    if HAVE_LP and hard_deadline - time.monotonic() > 700.0:
        n_lp = 512
        if parent is not None:
            lp_base = interp_sequence(parent, n_lp)
        else:
            lp_base = np.ones(n_lp, dtype=np.float64)

        lp_deadline = min(hard_deadline, time.monotonic() + 90.0)
        a_lp, sc_lp = lp_refine(lp_base, iters=18, deadline=lp_deadline)

        for n_out in (8192, 12288):
            if n_out in seen:
                continue
            if hard_deadline - time.monotonic() <= 350.0:
                break
            a = interp_sequence(a_lp, n_out)
            seeds.append((f"lp_{n_out}", a, max(90, int(0.10 * total))))
            seen.add(n_out)

    for n in (4096, 8192):
        if n in seen:
            continue
        if hard_deadline - time.monotonic() <= 350.0:
            break
        inits = build_initializations(n, rng, parent, limit=4)
        for idx, init in enumerate(inits[:2]):
            seeds.append((f"syn_{n}_{idx}", init, max(60, int(0.06 * total))))
            if len(seeds) > 10:
                break

    if not seeds:
        if parent is not None:
            a0 = interp_sequence(parent, 6144)
        else:
            a0 = np.ones(6144, dtype=np.float64)
        seeds.append(("default", a0, int(max(120, total))))

    cumulative = 0.0
    for label, a0, budget in seeds:
        budget = float(max(60, budget))
        cumulative += budget
        limit = min(hard_deadline, t0 + cumulative)

        if limit <= time.monotonic():
            continue

        if best_sc < 1.4990 and cumulative > 0.8 * total:
            break

        try:
            a_local, sc_local = optimize_sequence(a0, limit, rng, label)
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"{label} failed: {e}", flush=True)
            continue

        if np.isfinite(sc_local) and (best_a is None or sc_local < best_sc):
            best_sc = sc_local
            best_a = np.asarray(a_local, dtype=np.float64).copy()
            print(
                f"GLOBAL NEW BEST {best_sc:.8f} from {label} n={len(best_a)} "
                f"t={time.monotonic() - t0:.0f}s",
                flush=True
            )

        if time.monotonic() > hard_deadline - 60.0:
            break

    if best_a is None:
        best_a = np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64)

    out = safe_finalize(best_a)

    try:
        verified = float(evaluate_sequence(out))
    except Exception:
        verified = float(best_sc if best_a is not None else np.nan)

    print("=" * 60, flush=True)
    print(f"FINAL internal={float(best_sc):.8f} verified={verified:.8f} length={len(out)} time={time.monotonic() - t0:.1f}s", flush=True)
    print("=" * 60, flush=True)

    return out
```
